1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any speech module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 251. The projective resolution has length at most 511 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 251 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the speech structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 511. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
